# Preserve legacy WW1 crypto bonuses without affecting La Resistance cryptology.
endsieg_ww1_crypto_effects = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dlc = "La Resistance" }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_tech = endsieg_ww1_cipher_crypto
					has_tech = endsieg_ww1_teleprinter_crypto
				}
			}
			set_technology = {
				endsieg_ww1_cipher_crypto = 0
				endsieg_ww1_teleprinter_crypto = 0
				popup = no
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_tech = cipher_machine
				NOT = { has_tech = endsieg_ww1_cipher_crypto }
			}
			set_technology = { endsieg_ww1_cipher_crypto = 1 popup = no }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_tech = teleprinter
				NOT = { has_tech = endsieg_ww1_teleprinter_crypto }
			}
			set_technology = { endsieg_ww1_teleprinter_crypto = 1 popup = no }
		}
	}
}

# Invite historical co-belligerents from Britain, never the other way round.
endsieg_join_historical_allies = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { date < 1936.1.1 }
			date < 1946.1.1
			country_exists = ENG
			NOT = { tag = ENG }
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ENG }
			ENG = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SOV }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		ENG = { add_to_faction = PREV }
	}
}

# Repair inherited Comintern membership once, then enact Turkey's dated war entry.
endsieg_TUR_historical_alignment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = TUR
			NOT = { date < 1936.1.1 }
			date < 1946.1.1
			is_subject = no
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				date < 1945.2.23
				NOT = { has_country_flag = endsieg_TUR_pre_entry_alignment_checked }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction_with = SOV }
				leave_faction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { country_exists = GER has_war_with = GER }
					GER = { white_peace = PREV }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { country_exists = JAP has_war_with = JAP }
					JAP = { white_peace = PREV }
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = endsieg_TUR_pre_entry_alignment_checked
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { date < 1945.2.23 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = endsieg_TUR_historical_allied_entry }
			}
			endsieg_join_historical_allies = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction_with = ENG }
				if = {
					limit = {
						country_exists = GER
						GER = { has_capitulated = no }
						NOT = { has_war_with = GER }
					}
					declare_war_on = { target = GER type = annex_everything }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						country_exists = JAP
						JAP = { has_capitulated = no }
						NOT = { has_war_with = JAP }
					}
					declare_war_on = { target = JAP type = annex_everything }
				}
				set_country_flag = endsieg_TUR_historical_allied_entry
			}
		}
	}
}

#This should only be used for AXIS vs ALLIES, or JAPAN vs ALLIES
declare_war_on_all_allies = {
	if = {
		#Make sure we dont declare war on UK a second time
		limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = ENG } }
		log = "[?ROOT.GetName] was NOT at war with ENG"
		declare_war_on = { target = ENG type = annex_everything }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		ROOT_call_allies_both_sides = yes
	}
}

ROOT_call_allies_both_sides = {
	ROOT_call_all_allies_against_ENG = yes
	ENG_call_all_allies_against_ROOT = yes
}

ROOT_call_all_allies_against_ENG = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = ROOT.allies

		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = ROOT
			enemy = ENG
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = ROOT.subjects

		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = ROOT
			enemy = ENG
		}
	}
}

ENG_call_all_allies_against_ROOT = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
	    array = ENG.subjects

		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = ENG
			enemy = PREV
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
	    array = ENG.faction_members

		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = ENG
			enemy = PREV
		}
	}
}

# Vanilla 1.19 leaf effect used by retained African Union events.
set_african_central_bank_variables = {
	set_variable = { org_africa_central_bank_stability_factor = 0.05}
	set_variable = { org_africa_central_bank_industrial_complex_factor = 0.1}
	set_variable = { org_africa_central_bank_infrastructure_factor = 0.1}



}
indonesia_increase_escalation_1_effect = {
	hidden_effect = { INS = { add_to_variable = { var = INS_war_escalation_level value = 1 tooltip = INS_increase_escalation_1_tt } } }
	hidden_effect = { INS = { clamp_variable = { var = INS_war_escalation_level max = 10 } } }
	custom_effect_tooltip = INS_increase_escalation_1_tt
	indonesia_evaluate_escalation_effect = yes
}



# BEGIN GENERATED ENDSIEG CHARACTER LIFECYCLE
endsieg_character_activate_alb_turhan_permeti_a4997255 = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = ALB_Turhan_Permeti }
			ALB = { has_character = ALB_Turhan_Permeti }
		}
		ALB_Turhan_Permeti = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = ALB_Turhan_Permeti
			ALB_Turhan_Permeti = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { ALB_Turhan_Permeti = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_retire_alb_turhan_permeti_a4997255 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = ALB_Turhan_Permeti } }
		ALB_Turhan_Permeti = { endsieg_character_dormant_alb_turhan_permeti_a4997255 = yes }
	}
}

endsieg_character_dormant_alb_turhan_permeti_a4997255 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		owner = { if = { limit = { has_idea = ALB_Turhan_Permeti } deactivate_advisor = ALB_Turhan_Permeti } }
		if = { limit = { has_ideology = moderatism } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = moderatism } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = GER_INT_gustav_stresemann } }
		GER_INT_gustav_stresemann = { endsieg_character_dormant_ger_int_gustav_stresemann_2b80705a = yes }
	}
}

endsieg_character_dormant_ger_int_gustav_stresemann_2b80705a = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		owner = { if = { limit = { has_idea = GER_INT_gustav_stresemann } deactivate_advisor = GER_INT_gustav_stresemann } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

endsieg_character_activate_ger_int_hans_luther_10a400cd = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = GER_INT_hans_luther }
			GER = { has_character = GER_INT_hans_luther }
		}
		GER_INT_hans_luther = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = GER_INT_hans_luther
			GER_INT_hans_luther = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { GER_INT_hans_luther = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_activate_ger_adolf_hitler_441af86d = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = GER_adolf_hitler }
			GER = { has_character = GER_adolf_hitler }
		}
		GER_adolf_hitler = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = GER_adolf_hitler
			GER_adolf_hitler = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = GER_adolf_hitler
			country_leader = {
				ideology=nazism
				traits={ dictator }
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { GER_adolf_hitler = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_retire_ger_adolf_hitler_441af86d = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = GER_adolf_hitler } }
		GER_adolf_hitler = { endsieg_character_dormant_ger_adolf_hitler_441af86d = yes }
	}
}

endsieg_character_dormant_ger_adolf_hitler_441af86d = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_ideology = nazism } remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = nazism } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		owner = { if = { limit = { has_idea = SOV_Lev_Trotsky } deactivate_advisor = SOV_Lev_Trotsky } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

endsieg_character_activate_so2_nikolay_kuznetsov_9e9a290c = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = SO2_Nikolay_Kuznetsov }
			SOV = { has_character = SO2_Nikolay_Kuznetsov }
		}
		SO2_Nikolay_Kuznetsov = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = SO2_Nikolay_Kuznetsov
			SO2_Nikolay_Kuznetsov = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { SO2_Nikolay_Kuznetsov = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_retire_so2_nikolay_kuznetsov_9e9a290c = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = SO2_Nikolay_Kuznetsov } }
		SO2_Nikolay_Kuznetsov = { endsieg_character_dormant_so2_nikolay_kuznetsov_9e9a290c = yes }
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }

	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = SO2_Semyon_Budyonny }
			SOV = { has_character = SO2_Semyon_Budyonny }
		}
		SO2_Semyon_Budyonny = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = SO2_Semyon_Budyonny
			SO2_Semyon_Budyonny = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		add_corps_commander_role = {
			character = SO2_Semyon_Budyonny
			traits={ trickster }
			skill=2
			attack_skill=3
			defense_skill=3
			planning_skill=2
			logistics_skill=2
			legacy_id=4104
		}
		hidden_effect = { SO2_Semyon_Budyonny = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = SOV_mikhail_tukhachevsky } }
		SOV_mikhail_tukhachevsky = { endsieg_character_dormant_sov_mikhail_tukhachevsky_dc1f2784 = yes }
	}
}

endsieg_character_dormant_sov_mikhail_tukhachevsky_dc1f2784 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		owner = { if = { limit = { has_idea = mikhail_tukhachevsky } deactivate_advisor = mikhail_tukhachevsky } }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}

endsieg_character_activate_sov_nikandr_chibisov_26385c85 = {
	# PREV is the activation country inside the immediate character scope.
	hidden_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character = SOV_nikandr_chibisov }
			SOV = { has_character = SOV_nikandr_chibisov }
		}
		SOV_nikandr_chibisov = { set_nationality = PREV }
	}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
			has_character = SOV_nikandr_chibisov
			SOV_nikandr_chibisov = { NOT = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
			}
		}
		add_corps_commander_role = {
			character = SOV_nikandr_chibisov
			traits = { trickster }
			skill = 3
			attack_skill = 1
			defense_skill = 3
			planning_skill = 3
			logistics_skill = 3
			legacy_id = 0
			visible = { NOT = { has_character_flag = SOV_exiled_flag } }
		}
		hidden_effect = { SOV_nikandr_chibisov = { set_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
	}
}

endsieg_character_retire_sov_nikandr_chibisov_26385c85 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character = SOV_nikandr_chibisov } }
		SOV_nikandr_chibisov = { endsieg_character_dormant_sov_nikandr_chibisov_26385c85 = yes }
	}
}

endsieg_character_dormant_sov_nikandr_chibisov_26385c85 = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited } }
		if = { limit = { is_unit_leader = yes } remove_unit_leader_role = yes }
		hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = endsieg_character_recruited }
	}
}
